<script>(() => {
  const root = document.getElementById('jm-nav-widget');
  if (!root || root.dataset.ready) return;
  root.dataset.ready = 'true';
  const config = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('jm-navbar-settings').textContent);
  const home = new URL(config.homeUrl);
  const path = value => value.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
  const onHomepage = () => location.origin === home.origin && (path(location.pathname) === path(home.pathname) || (path(home.pathname) === '/homepage' && path(location.pathname) === '/'));
  const localPreview = () => ['localhost','127.0.0.1'].includes(location.hostname);
  const header = root.querySelector('.jmn-header');
  const spacer = document.getElementById('jm-nav-space');
  const nav = root.querySelector('#jm-navigation');
  const menu = root.querySelector('.jmn-menu');
  const quiz = root.querySelector('#jm-style-dialog');
  const booking = root.querySelector('#jm-booking-dialog');
  const content = root.querySelector('#jm-quiz-content');
  const form = root.querySelector('#jm-reservation-form');
  const status = root.querySelector('#jm-form-status');
  const success = root.querySelector('#jm-booking-success');
  let step = 0, answers = [], quizResult = null, sending = false;
  let previousOverflow = '';
  // Escape GHL ancestors that apply transforms or clipping to Custom Code elements.
  document.body.appendChild(root);
  const resize = new ResizeObserver(() => { spacer.style.height = header.offsetHeight + 'px'; });
  resize.observe(header);
  spacer.style.height = header.offsetHeight + 'px';
  function closeMenu() {
    nav.classList.remove('is-open'); menu.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    menu.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Open navigation menu'); menu.textContent = '☰';
  }
  menu.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const open = nav.classList.toggle('is-open');
    menu.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
    menu.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'Close navigation menu' : 'Open navigation menu');
    menu.textContent = open ? '×' : '☰';
  });
  const headings = {experiences:'More Than Shopping — An Experience',about:'Meet Juanita Michelle',visit:'Visit & Contact',contact:'Visit & Contact'};
  function section(key) {
    // Never mistake a shop footer anchor for a homepage section.
    if (!onHomepage() && !localPreview()) return null;
    // IDs are editable for native GHL sections; heading lookup also supports the exported homepage.
    let target;
    try { target = document.querySelector(config.sections[key]); } catch { return null; }
    if (!target) target = [...document.querySelectorAll('h2')].find(h => h.textContent.trim() === headings[key])?.closest('section');
    return target;
  }
  function jump(key, updateHash = true) {
    const target = section(key);
    if (!target) return false;
    closeMenu();
    const top = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - header.offsetHeight - 16;
    window.scrollTo({top: Math.max(0, top), behavior: matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ? 'instant' : 'smooth'});
    if (updateHash) history.replaceState(null, '', '#' + key);
    nav.querySelectorAll('[data-section]').forEach(link => {
      if (link.dataset.section === key) link.setAttribute('aria-current', 'location');
      else link.removeAttribute('aria-current');
    });
    return true;
  }
  const normalClick = event => !event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey && !event.shiftKey && !event.altKey && event.button === 0;
  function navigate(event, url) {
    if (!normalClick(event)) return;
    event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); closeMenu();
    window.location.assign(url);
  }
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-home]').forEach(link => {
    link.href = home.href;
    link.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (!normalClick(event)) return;
      if (onHomepage()) {
        event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();closeMenu();
        history.replaceState(null,'',home.pathname + home.search);
        window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});
        nav.querySelectorAll('[aria-current]').forEach(item=>item.removeAttribute('aria-current'));
        nav.querySelector('[data-home]')?.setAttribute('aria-current','page');
      } else navigate(event,home.href);
    },true);
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-shop]').forEach(link => {
    link.href = config.shopUrl;
    link.addEventListener('click', event => navigate(event,link.href),true);
  });
  if (onHomepage() && !location.hash) nav.querySelector('[data-home]')?.setAttribute('aria-current','page');
  if (path(location.pathname) === path(new URL(config.shopUrl).pathname)) nav.querySelector('[data-shop]')?.setAttribute('aria-current','page');
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-section]').forEach(link => {
    link.href = home.href.split('#')[0] + '#' + link.dataset.section;
    link.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (!normalClick(event)) return;
      event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
      nav.querySelectorAll('[data-home],[data-shop]').forEach(item=>item.removeAttribute('aria-current'));
      if (!jump(link.dataset.section)) window.location.assign(link.href);
    },true);
  });
  // Revisit hash targets after the homepage renderer and images finish loading.
  const followHash = () => {
    const key = location.hash.slice(1);
    if (Object.hasOwn(config.sections, key)) return jump(key, false);
    return false;
  };
  let hashFrame = 0;
  const queueHash = () => {
    cancelAnimationFrame(hashFrame);
    hashFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(followHash));
  };
  // GHL and the homepage renderer can insert content after the initial page load.
  // Keep watching briefly so their initial scroll resets do not override the destination.
  const sectionObserver = new MutationObserver(queueHash);
  sectionObserver.observe(document.body, {childList:true,subtree:true});
  queueHash();
  setTimeout(() => sectionObserver.disconnect(), 10000);
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', followHash);
  window.addEventListener('load', queueHash, {once:true});

  function openDialog(dialog) {
    closeMenu();
    if (!quiz.open && !booking.open) { previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow; document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; }
    dialog.showModal();
  }
  [quiz, booking].forEach(dialog => {
    dialog.querySelectorAll('[data-close]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => dialog.close()));
    dialog.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (event.target !== dialog) return;
      const r = dialog.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (event.clientX < r.left || event.clientX > r.right || event.clientY < r.top || event.clientY > r.bottom) dialog.close();
    });
    dialog.addEventListener('close', () => {
      if (!quiz.open && !booking.open) document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
    });
  });
  function openBooking(fromQuiz) {
    if (!sending) {
      form.hidden = false; success.hidden = true; status.hidden = true;
      form.elements.experience_type.value = fromQuiz ? quizResult.service : 'Sip & Shop Experience';
      if (fromQuiz) {
        form.elements.favorite_color.value = answers[0];
        form.elements.preferred_accent.value = answers[1];
      }
    }
    openDialog(booking);
  }
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-open]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (button.dataset.open === 'quiz') { renderQuiz(); openDialog(quiz); }
    else openBooking(false);
  }));
  const questions = [
    {text:'What color family usually makes you feel most like yourself?', options:[
      ['Deep Burgundy & Rich Wine','#4c1927','Glamour & Warmth'],['Champagne Gold & Warm Neutrals','#d4af37','Elegance & Polish'],
      ['Bold Jewel Tones (Emerald, Sapphire)','#1b4332','Vibrant Statement'],['Classic Black & Monochromatic','#1a1a1a','Chic Edge']]},
    {text:"When completing an outfit, what's usually missing?",options:[
      ['The statement hat or brim to crown the look'],['Distinctive earrings, necklace, or cuff jewelry'],
      ['A unique handbag or custom finishing accessory'],['A signature personal fragrance that lingers']]},
    {text:'How do you prefer to shop for key pieces?',options:[
      ['In an intimate, fun Sip & Shop atmosphere with friends'],['One-on-one with Juanita guiding every step'],
      ['Hands-on crafting my own custom accessories'],['Quick personalized recommendations delivered or prepped']]}
  ];
  const personalities = [
    {title:'The Crown Accent Specialist',service:'Custom Hat Bar Workshop',description:'You have effortless poise! Your look gains maximum impact when crowned with a custom fedora or brim tailored with luxury ribbons, feathers, and gold hardware.',items:['Custom Distressed Fedora','Layered Statement Necklaces','Signature Leather Handbag']},
    {title:'The Statement Jewelry Muse',service:'Jewelry Accent Styling',description:'You bring a look to life through distinctive details. Sculptural earrings, layered necklaces, and polished cuffs give your personal style a confident finishing touch.',items:['Statement Earrings','Layered Necklaces','Sculptural Gold Cuffs']},
    {title:'The Curated Accessory Connoisseur',service:'Curated Accessory Consultation',description:'You appreciate an accessory that tells a story. A beautifully structured handbag and thoughtfully chosen accents make your everyday look feel distinctly yours.',items:['Signature Leather Handbag','Statement Accent Scarf','Distinctive Clutch']},
    {title:'Sensory Signature Elegance',service:'Perfume Blend Studio',description:'Your style is subtle yet unforgettable. A personal fragrance and thoughtfully layered finishing touches leave a lasting impression.',items:['Bespoke Perfume Blend','Chunky Gold Cuffs','Velvet Accent Clutch']}
  ];
  const escape = value => String(value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  function renderQuiz(focus = false) {
    if (step < 3) {
      const q = questions[step];
      content.innerHTML = `<div class="jmn-progress"><span>Question ${step+1} of 3</span><span class="jmn-dots" aria-hidden="true">${questions.map((_,i)=>`<i class="${i===step?'active':''}"></i>`).join('')}</span></div>
        <h3 class="jmn-question" tabindex="-1">${escape(q.text)}</h3><div class="jmn-options">${q.options.map((opt,i)=>
          `<button type="button" class="jmn-option" data-answer="${i}" aria-pressed="${answers[step]===opt[0]}">${opt[1]?`<span class="jmn-swatch" style="background:${opt[1]}" aria-hidden="true"></span>`:''}<span class="jmn-option-label">${escape(opt[0])}</span>${opt[2]?`<span class="jmn-tag">${escape(opt[2])}</span>`:''}</button>`).join('')}</div>${step>0?'<button type="button" class="jmn-back">← Previous question</button>':''}`;
      content.querySelectorAll('[data-answer]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        answers[step] = q.options[Number(button.dataset.answer)][0]; step++; renderQuiz(true);
      }));
      content.querySelector('.jmn-back')?.addEventListener('click', () => { step--; renderQuiz(true); });
    } else {
      const index = questions[1].options.findIndex(opt => opt[0] === answers[1]);
      quizResult = personalities[index] || personalities[0];
      content.innerHTML = `<div class="jmn-result"><span class="jmn-result-label">Your Style Personality</span><h3 tabindex="-1">${escape(quizResult.title)}</h3><p>${escape(quizResult.description)}</p>
        <div class="jmn-recommendations"><div class="jmn-recommendations-title"><span>✧ RECOMMENDED FINISHING ACCENTS:</span><span class="jmn-tag">${escape(answers[0])}</span></div><ul>${quizResult.items.map(item=>`<li>${escape(item)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>
        <div class="jmn-result-actions"><button type="button" class="jmn-button jmn-gold" data-result-book>Book ${escape(quizResult.service)} →</button><button type="button" class="jmn-button jmn-outline" data-retake>↻ Retake Quiz</button></div></div>`;
      content.querySelector('[data-result-book]').addEventListener('click', () => {
        // Open the next dialog first so body scroll stays locked during the handoff.
        openBooking(true); quiz.close();
      });
      content.querySelector('[data-retake]').addEventListener('click', () => { step=0; answers=[]; quizResult=null; renderQuiz(true); });
    }
    quiz.scrollTop = 0;
    if (focus) content.querySelector('h3')?.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }
  const displayError = message => { status.textContent = message; status.hidden = false; };
  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (sending || !form.reportValidity()) return;
    if (!config.reservationEndpoint) {
      displayError('Online reservation requests are not connected yet. Please call 347-601-8884 to arrange your experience.');
      return;
    }
    let endpoint;
    try { endpoint = new URL(config.reservationEndpoint); if(endpoint.protocol !== 'https:') throw new Error(); }
    catch { displayError('Online reservations are temporarily unavailable. Please call 347-601-8884.'); return; }
    const payload = {
      event:'juanita.reservation.requested',schema_version:1,
      ...Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)),
      style_personality:quizResult?.title || '',
      recommended_experience:quizResult?.service || '',
      quiz_color:answers[0] || '',quiz_accent:answers[1] || '',quiz_shopping_preference:answers[2] || '',
      source:'boutique_navbar',page_url:location.href,submitted_at:new Date().toISOString()
    };
    sending=true; status.hidden=true;
    const submit = form.querySelector('[type=submit]');
    submit.disabled=true; submit.textContent='Sending your request…';
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 20000);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(endpoint.href, {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(payload),signal:controller.signal,credentials:'omit'});
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Request rejected');
      form.hidden=true; success.hidden=false; success.setAttribute('tabindex','-1');success.focus();
      form.reset(); step=0; answers=[]; quizResult=null;
    } catch {
      displayError('We could not confirm your request was received. Your details are still here. Please call 347-601-8884 before trying again.');
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);sending=false;submit.disabled=false;submit.textContent='Submit VIP Reservation Request →';
    }
  });
})();
</script>

      rel="stylesheet"

<script>(function(){const host=document.getElementById('jm-site-root');const spacer=document.createElement('div');spacer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');function update(){const header=host.querySelector('header');if(!header)return;if(!spacer.parentNode)header.after(spacer);spacer.style.height=header.offsetHeight+'px';const w=document.documentElement.clientWidth;host.style.width=w+'px';host.style.maxWidth='none';host.style.marginLeft='calc(50% - '+w/2+'px)';host.style.marginRight='calc(50% - '+w/2+'px)';}const observer=new MutationObserver(update);observer.observe(host,{childList:true,subtree:true});const resize=new ResizeObserver(update);resize.observe(document.documentElement);addEventListener('resize',update);update();})();</script>
